## `AUS-2420-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AUS` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Potline-Smelter Operator
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Aluminum Smelting Reduction Cell & Anode Changing
